## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 2 page(s) - 3 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [region](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/region?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate
**Issue**: All page content should be contained by landmarks

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<div class="carousel-item active">
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
.active.carousel-item
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('.active.carousel-item')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://www.oudergem.be/
- https://vorst.brussels/nl

---

#### Pattern 2: Affects 2 page(s) - 2 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [aria-dialog-name](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/aria-dialog-name?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**Issue**: ARIA dialog and alertdialog nodes should have an accessible name

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<div id="sliding-popup" role="alertdialog" aria-describedby="popup-text" class="sliding-popup-bottom" style="height: auto; width: 100%; bottom: 0px;">
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
#sliding-popup
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('#sliding-popup')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://www.anderlecht.be/
- https://etterbeek.brussels/nl

---

#### Pattern 3: Affects 2 page(s) - 2 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [color-contrast](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/color-contrast?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**Issue**: Elements must meet minimum color contrast ratio thresholds

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<button type="button" class="decline-button eu-cookie-compliance-default-button" tabindex="0">Weigeren</button>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
.decline-button
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('.decline-button')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://etterbeek.brussels/nl
- https://vorst.brussels/nl

---

> 💡 **Pro Tip**: These patterns likely come from shared components or templates in your codebase. Fix them in the component/template source, and the fix will propagate to all affected pages.
